In France, however, the organization of training sessions is limited by human and logistical constraints, restricting students' access to repeated practice and structured feedback. Recent advances in Natural Language Processing (NLP) and Large Language Models (LLMs) now offer the opportunity to automatically evaluate such medical interviews, thereby alleviating the need for human examiners during training. Yet, real French OSCE annotated transcripts remain extremely scarce, limiting reproducible research and reliable benchmarking. To address these challenges, we investigate the use of LLMs for both generating and evaluating French OSCE dialogues in a low-resource context. We introduce a controlled pipeline that produces synthetic doctor-patient interview transcripts guided by scenario-specific evaluation criteria, combining ideal and perturbed performances to simulate varying student skill levels. The resulting dialogues are automatically silver-labeled through an LLM-assisted framework supporting adjustable evaluation strictness. Benchmarking multiple open-source and proprietary LLMs shows that mid-size models ($\le$32B parameters) achieve accuracies comparable to GPT-4o ($\sim$90\%) on synthetic data, highlighting the feasibility of locally deployable, privacy-preserving evaluation systems for medical education.

</details>


### [41] [Clickbait detection: quick inference with maximum impact](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.08148)
*Soveatin Kuntur,Panggih Kusuma Ningrum,Anna Wróblewska,Maria Ganzha,Marcin Paprzycki*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 提出了一种轻量级混合点击诱饵检测方法，结合OpenAI语义嵌入和六种紧凑启发式特征，通过PCA降维和多种分类器评估，在保持竞争力的同时显著降低推理时间。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 开发一个高效的点击诱饵检测系统，能够在保持检测性能的同时显著降低计算成本和推理时间，适用于实际应用场景。

Method: 采用混合方法：结合OpenAI语义嵌入（捕捉语义信息）和六种紧凑的启发式特征（捕捉风格和信息线索）。使用PCA对嵌入进行降维以提高效率，并使用XGBoost、GraphSAGE和GCN三种分类器进行评估。

Result: 简化特征设计导致F1分数略有下降，但基于图的模型（GraphSAGE和GCN）实现了具有竞争力的性能，同时显著减少了推理时间。高ROC-AUC值表明模型具有很强的区分能力，支持在不同决策阈值下可靠检测点击诱饵标题。

Conclusion: 提出的轻量级混合方法在保持检测性能的同时显著提高了效率，基于图的模型在性能与推理时间之间取得了良好平衡，高ROC-AUC值确保了模型在不同阈值下的可靠性，为实际部署提供了可行的解决方案。

Abstract: We propose a lightweight hybrid approach to clickbait detection that combines OpenAI semantic embeddings with six compact heuristic features capturing stylistic and informational cues. To improve efficiency, embeddings are reduced using PCA and evaluated with XGBoost, GraphSAGE, and GCN classifiers. While the simplified feature design yields slightly lower F1-scores, graph-based models achieve competitive performance with substantially reduced inference time. High ROC--AUC values further indicate strong discrimination capability, supporting reliable detection of clickbait headlines under varying decision thresholds.

</details>


### [42] [Training Data Size Sensitivity in Unsupervised Rhyme Recognition](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.08156)
*Petr Plecháč,Artjoms Šeļa,Silvie Cinková,Mirella De Sisto,Lara Nugues,Neža Kočnik,Antonina Martynenko,Ben Nagy,Luca Giovannini,Robert Kolár*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 该研究探讨了无监督押韵识别所需训练数据量，使用RhymeTagger工具在七种语言中评估性能，发现足够训练数据下RhymeTagger优于人类标注一致性，而缺乏语音表征的大语言模型在此任务上表现不佳。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 押韵识别具有挑战性：押韵定义具有历史建构性，学者对押韵分类存在分歧，人们对于两个词是否押韵意见不一，这使多语言环境下的自动押韵识别和评估变得复杂。

Method: 使用语言无关工具RhymeTagger，基于诗歌语料库中的重复模式识别押韵；在七种语言（捷克语、德语、英语、法语、意大利语、俄语、斯洛文尼亚语）中评估性能；分析训练数据量和语言差异对准确性的影响；建立人工标注子集评估标注者间一致性；分析导致专家标注分歧的因素（押韵词语音相似性和诗中距离）；使用单样本学习策略将RhymeTagger与三个大语言模型进行比较。

Result: 一旦提供足够的训练数据，RhymeTagger在押韵识别任务上持续优于人类标注一致性；缺乏语音表征的大语言模型在此任务上显著困难；训练数据量和语言差异对RhymeTagger性能有重要影响。

Conclusion: RhymeTagger在获得足够训练数据时能够可靠地进行无监督押韵识别，且性能超过人类标注一致性；大语言模型由于缺乏语音表征，在押韵识别任务上表现不佳；研究为多语言押韵识别建立了现实性能基准。

Abstract: Rhyme is deceptively intuitive: what is or is not a rhyme is constructed historically, scholars struggle with rhyme classification, and people disagree on whether two words are rhymed or not. This complicates automated rhymed recognition and evaluation, especially in multilingual context. This article investigates how much training data is needed for reliable unsupervised rhyme recognition using RhymeTagger, a language-independent tool that identifies rhymes based on repeating patterns in poetry corpora. We evaluate its performance across seven languages (Czech, German, English, French, Italian, Russian, and Slovene), examining how training size and language differences affect accuracy. To set a realistic performance benchmark, we assess inter-annotator agreement on a manually annotated subset of poems and analyze factors contributing to disagreement in expert annotations: phonetic similarity between rhyming words and their distance from each other in a poem. We also compare RhymeTagger to three large language models using a one-shot learning strategy. Our findings show that, once provided with sufficient training data, RhymeTagger consistently outperforms human agreement, while LLMs lacking phonetic representation significantly struggle with the task.

</details>


### [43] [Self-Debias: Self-correcting for Debiasing Large Language Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.08243)
*Xuan Feng,Shuai Zhao,Luwei Xiao,Tianlong Gu,Bo An*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: Self-Debias是一种渐进式框架，通过将去偏过程重新定义为战略资源再分配问题，将模型输出概率质量视为有限资源，从有偏启发式重新分配到无偏推理路径，实现内在自我纠正能力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 尽管大语言模型展现出卓越的推理能力，但其内在的社会偏见经常在思维链过程中级联传播，导致持续的"偏见传播"。现有的去偏方法主要关注静态约束或外部干预，一旦偏见被触发，无法识别和中断这种传播过程。

Method: 将去偏过程重新定义为战略资源再分配问题，将模型输出概率质量视为有限资源，从有偏启发式重新分配到无偏推理路径。采用细粒度轨迹级目标，受动态去偏约束，使模型能够选择性修正有偏推理后缀，同时保留有效的上下文前缀。集成在线自我改进机制，利用一致性过滤自主合成监督信号。

Result: 仅使用20k标注样本，Self-Debias就能激活高效的自我纠正，在保持一般推理能力的同时实现优越的去偏性能，无需持续的外部监督。

Conclusion: Self-Debias通过将去偏重新定义为资源再分配问题，结合细粒度轨迹级优化和在线自我改进机制，成功解决了思维链过程中的偏见传播问题，实现了内在的自我纠正能力，为LLM去偏提供了新的有效方法。

Abstract: Although Large Language Models (LLMs) demonstrate remarkable reasoning capabilities, inherent social biases often cascade throughout the Chain-of-Thought (CoT) process, leading to continuous "Bias Propagation". Existing debiasing methods primarily focus on static constraints or external interventions, failing to identify and interrupt this propagation once triggered. To address this limitation, we introduce Self-Debias, a progressive framework designed to instill intrinsic self-correction capabilities. Specifically, we reformulate the debiasing process as a strategic resource redistribution problem, treating the model's output probability mass as a limited resource to be reallocated from biased heuristics to unbiased reasoning paths. Unlike standard preference optimization which applies broad penalties, Self-Debias employs a fine-grained trajectory-level objective subject to dynamic debiasing constraints. This enables the model to selectively revise biased reasoning suffixes while preserving valid contextual prefixes. Furthermore, we integrate an online self-improvement mechanism utilizing consistency filtering to autonomously synthesize supervision signals. With merely 20k annotated samples, Self-Debias activates efficient self-correction, achieving superior debiasing performance while preserving general reasoning capabilities without continuous external oversight.

</details>


### [44] [HyperMem: Hypergraph Memory for Long-Term Conversations](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.08256)
*Juwei Yue,Chuanrui Hu,Jiawei Sheng,Zuyi Zhou,Wenyuan Zhang,Tingwen Liu,Li Guo,Yafeng Deng*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 提出HyperMem：基于超图的层次化记忆架构，通过超边显式建模多元素间的高阶关联，解决传统方法在长对话中记忆碎片化问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有对话记忆方法（如RAG和图记忆）主要依赖成对关系，难以捕捉多个元素间的高阶关联，导致记忆检索碎片化，影响长对话的连贯性、任务追踪和个性化交互。

Method: 提出超图层次记忆架构：1）三层结构（主题、情节、事实）；2）通过超边将相关情节及其事实分组为连贯单元；3）设计混合词义索引和粗到细检索策略，支持高阶关联的准确高效检索。

Result: 在LoCoMo基准测试中达到92.73%的LLM-as-a-judge准确率，实现最先进性能，证明HyperMem在长对话中的有效性。

Conclusion: HyperMem通过超图建模高阶关联，解决了长对话记忆的碎片化问题，显著提升了对话连贯性和记忆检索质量。

Abstract: Long-term memory is essential for conversational agents to maintain coherence, track persistent tasks, and provide personalized interactions across extended dialogues. However, existing approaches as Retrieval-Augmented Generation (RAG) and graph-based memory mostly rely on pairwise relations, which can hardly capture high-order associations, i.e., joint dependencies among multiple elements, causing fragmented retrieval. To this end, we propose HyperMem, a hypergraph-based hierarchical memory architecture that explicitly models such associations using hyperedges. Particularly, HyperMem structures memory into three levels: topics, episodes, and facts, and groups related episodes and their facts via hyperedges, unifying scattered content into coherent units. Leveraging this structure, we design a hybrid lexical-semantic index and a coarse-to-fine retrieval strategy, supporting accurate and efficient retrieval of high-order associations. Experiments on the LoCoMo benchmark show that HyperMem achieves state-of-the-art performance with 92.73% LLM-as-a-judge accuracy, demonstrating the effectiveness of HyperMem for long-term conversations.

</details>


### [45] [Floating or Suggesting Ideas? A Large-Scale Contrastive Analysis of Metaphorical and Literal Verb-Object Constructions](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.08275)
*Prisca Piccirilli,Alexander Fraser,Sabine Schulte im Walde*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 大规模语料分析显示，隐喻与字面表达在动词-宾语搭配中的差异具有构造特异性，而非统一模式


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 隐喻在日常语言中普遍存在，但大规模比较隐喻与字面语言的研究有限，特别是对于近义表达。本研究旨在通过大规模语料分析，系统比较隐喻与字面表达在动词-宾语搭配中的分布特征。

Method: 分析297个英语动词-宾语对（如float idea vs. suggest idea），在约200万语料句子中考察其上下文使用。使用五种NLP工具提取2,293个认知和语言特征，涵盖情感、词汇、句法和语篇层面属性。进行跨对分析（比较隐喻与字面语境）和内部对分析（考察单个VO对内部差异）。

Result: 跨对分析显示：字面语境具有更高的词汇频率、连贯性和结构规律性；隐喻语境则表现出更大的情感负荷、意象性、词汇多样性和构造特异性。内部对分析揭示显著异质性，大多数VO对表现出非均匀效应，没有单一一致的分布模式能区分隐喻与字面使用。

Conclusion: 隐喻与字面表达的差异主要是构造特异性的，而非统一模式。大规模数据结合多样化特征为理解VO搭配中的隐喻-字面对比提供了细粒度视角。

Abstract: Metaphor pervades everyday language, allowing speakers to express abstract concepts via concrete domains. While prior work has studied metaphors cognitively and psycholinguistically, large-scale comparisons with literal language remain limited, especially for near-synonymous expressions. We analyze 297 English verb-object pairs (e.g., float idea vs. suggest idea) in ~2M corpus sentences, examining their contextual usage. Using five NLP tools, we extract 2,293 cognitive and linguistic features capturing affective, lexical, syntactic, and discourse-level properties. We address: (i) whether features differ between metaphorical and literal contexts (cross-pair analysis), and (ii) whether individual VO pairs diverge internally (within-pair analysis). Cross-pair results show literal contexts have higher lexical frequency, cohesion, and structural regularity, while metaphorical contexts show greater affective load, imageability, lexical diversity, and constructional specificity. Within-pair analyses reveal substantial heterogeneity, with most pairs showing non-uniform effects. These results suggest no single, consistent distributional pattern that distinguishes metaphorical from literal usage. Instead, differences are largely construction-specific. Overall, large-scale data combined with diverse features provides a fine-grained understanding of metaphor-literal contrasts in VO usage.

</details>


### [46] [When to Trust Tools? Adaptive Tool Trust Calibration For Tool-Integrated Math Reasoning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.08281)
*Ruotao Xu,Yixin Ji,Yu Luo,Jinpeng Li,Dong Li,Peifeng Li,Juntao Li,Min Zhang*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: ATTC框架通过基于代码块置信度自适应选择信任或忽略工具结果，有效解决了TIR模型中的"工具忽略"问题，在多个数据集上实现了4.1%-7.5%的性能提升。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 大型推理模型在需要精确计算和广泛知识储备的任务中仍有不足，工具集成推理成为有前景的范式。然而现有开源TIR模型存在关键缺陷：当模型推理与工具结果冲突时，模型倾向于相信自身推理，即使工具结果正确也会被忽略，导致"工具忽略"问题，表明模型不知道何时信任或忽略工具。

Method: 提出自适应工具信任校准框架，通过基于生成的代码块的置信度分数，引导模型自适应地选择信任或忽略工具结果。

Result: 在不同规模和多个数据集上的实验结果表明，ATTC有效减少了"工具忽略"问题，性能提升了4.1%到7.5%。

Conclusion: ATTC框架通过校准模型对工具结果的信任度，解决了TIR模型中的关键缺陷，显著提升了模型性能，为工具集成推理提供了有效的信任校准机制。

Abstract: Large reasoning models (LRMs) have achieved strong performance enhancement through scaling test time computation, but due to the inherent limitations of the underlying language models, they still have shortcomings in tasks that require precise computation and extensive knowledge reserves. Tool-Integrated Reasoning (TIR) has emerged as a promising paradigm that incorporates tool call and execution within the reasoning trajectory. Although recent works have released some powerful open-source TIR models, our analysis reveals that these models still suffer from critical deficiencies. We find that when the reasoning of the model conflicts with the tool results, the model tends to believe in its own reasoning. And there are cases where the tool results are correct but are ignored by the model, resulting in incorrect answers, which we define as "Tool Ignored''. This indicates that the model does not know when to trust or ignore the tool. To overcome these limitations, We introduce Adaptive Tool Trust Calibration (ATTC), a novel framework that guides the model to adaptively choose to trust or ignore the tool results based on the confidence score of generated code blocks. The experimental results from various open-source TIR models of different sizes and across multiple datasets demonstrate that ATTC effectively reduces the "Tool Ignored" issue, resulting in a performance increase of 4.1% to 7.5%.

</details>


### [47] [Distributed Multi-Layer Editing for Rule-Level Knowledge in Large Language Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.08284)
*Yating Wang,Wenting Zhao,Yaqi Zhao,Yongshun Gong,Yilong Yin,Haoliang Sun*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 该论文研究大语言模型中规则级知识编辑问题，提出分布式多层编辑方法DMLE，相比传统事实级编辑方法显著提升规则一致性和理解能力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有模型编辑方法主要针对事实级知识，假设通过局部干预即可实现目标编辑。然而，规则级知识需要在多个相互依赖的形式（符号表达式、自然语言解释、具体实例）之间保持一致，这种假设不成立。因此需要研究规则级知识的机制化编辑方法。

Method: 首先扩展RuleEdit基准至200个手动验证的数学和物理规则。通过细粒度因果追踪分析规则知识在Transformer层中的组织形式，发现公式和描述集中在早期层，而实例更多与中间层关联。基于此洞察，提出分布式多层编辑（DMLE）方法：对公式和描述应用共享的早期层更新，对实例应用单独的中间层更新。

Result: DMLE在保持标准编辑指标竞争力的同时，显著提升规则级编辑性能。在GPT-J-6B、Qwen2.5-7B、Qwen2-7B和LLaMA-3-8B模型上，平均将实例可移植性和规则理解分别提升13.91和50.19个百分点。细粒度因果追踪揭示了规则知识的形式特异性组织模式。

Conclusion: 规则知识在Transformer层中不是均匀分布的，因此不能通过单层或连续块干预可靠编辑。DMLE通过分布式多层编辑策略有效解决了规则级知识编辑的挑战，为复杂知识结构的模型编辑提供了新思路。

Abstract: Large language models store not only isolated facts but also rules that support reasoning across symbolic expressions, natural language explanations, and concrete instances. Yet most model editing methods are built for fact-level knowledge, assuming that a target edit can be achieved through a localized intervention. This assumption does not hold for rule-level knowledge, where a single rule must remain consistent across multiple interdependent forms. We investigate this problem through a mechanistic study of rule-level knowledge editing. To support this study, we extend the RuleEdit benchmark from 80 to 200 manually verified rules spanning mathematics and physics. Fine-grained causal tracing reveals a form-specific organization of rule knowledge in transformer layers: formulas and descriptions are concentrated in earlier layers, while instances are more associated with middle layers. These results suggest that rule knowledge is not uniformly localized, and therefore cannot be reliably edited by a single-layer or contiguous-block intervention. Based on this insight, we propose Distributed Multi-Layer Editing (DMLE), which applies a shared early-layer update to formulas and descriptions and a separate middle-layer update to instances. While remaining competitive on standard editing metrics, DMLE achieves substantially stronger rule-level editing performance. On average, it improves instance portability and rule understanding by 13.91 and 50.19 percentage points, respectively, over the strongest baseline across GPT-J-6B, Qwen2.5-7B, Qwen2-7B, and LLaMA-3-8B. The code is available at https://github.com/Pepper66/DMLE.

</details>


### [48] [SeLaR: Selective Latent Reasoning in Large Language Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.08299)
*Renyu Fu,Guibo Luo*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: SeLaR通过选择性激活软嵌入和熵感知对比正则化，解决现有潜在推理方法中全局激活扰动高置信度步骤和软嵌入快速坍缩的问题，在五个推理基准上优于标准CoT和最先进的无训练方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有潜在推理方法存在两个主要问题：1) 全局激活会在高置信度推理步骤中引入扰动，损害推理稳定性；2) 软嵌入会快速坍缩向最高概率token，限制了替代推理轨迹的探索。需要一种轻量级、无需训练的方法来克服这些限制。

Method: 提出SeLaR框架，包含两个核心组件：1) 熵门控机制，仅在低置信度步骤激活软嵌入，在高置信度步骤保留离散解码；2) 熵感知对比正则化，将软嵌入推离主导(最高概率)token的方向，鼓励持续探索多个潜在推理路径。

Result: 在五个推理基准测试上的实验表明，SeLaR在推理性能上一致优于标准CoT和当前最先进的无训练方法。

Conclusion: SeLaR通过选择性激活软嵌入和熵感知对比正则化，有效解决了现有潜在推理方法的局限性，为大型语言模型的推理能力提供了更稳定和探索性的框架。

Abstract: Chain-of-Thought (CoT) has become a cornerstone of reasoning in large language models, yet its effectiveness is constrained by the limited expressiveness of discrete token sampling. Recent latent reasoning approaches attempt to alleviate this limitation by replacing discrete tokens with soft embeddings (probability-weighted mixtures of token embeddings) or hidden states, but they commonly suffer from two issues: (1) global activation injects perturbations into high-confidence steps, impairing reasoning stability; and (2) soft embeddings quickly collapse toward the highest-probability token, limiting exploration of alternative trajectories. To address these challenges, we propose SeLaR (Selective Latent Reasoning), a lightweight and training-free framework. SeLaR introduces an entropy-gated mechanism that activates soft embeddings only at low-confidence steps, while preserving discrete decoding at high-confidence steps. Additionally, we propose an entropy-aware contrastive regularization that pushes soft embeddings away from the dominant (highest-probability) token's direction, encouraging sustained exploration of multiple latent reasoning paths. Experiments on five reasoning benchmarks demonstrate that SeLaR consistently outperforms standard CoT and state-of-the-art training-free methods.

</details>


### [49] [Towards Real-world Human Behavior Simulation: Benchmarking Large Language Models on Long-horizon, Cross-scenario, Heterogeneous Behavior Traces](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.08362)
*Jiawei Chen,Ruoxi Xu,Boxi Cao,Ruotong Pan,Yunfei Zhang,Yifei Hu,Yong Du,Tingting Gao,Yaojie Lu,Yingfei Sun,Xianpei Han,Le Sun,Xiangyu Wu,Hongyu Lin*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: OmniBehavior是首个完全基于真实世界数据构建的用户模拟基准，揭示了现有LLM在模拟复杂人类行为时的局限性，特别是存在结构性偏见导致个体差异和长尾行为丢失。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有用户模拟基准局限于孤立场景、狭窄动作空间或合成数据，无法捕捉真实人类行为的整体性。需要构建一个能整合长时程、跨场景和异质行为模式的统一框架来弥合这一差距。

Method: 引入OmniBehavior基准，完全基于真实世界数据构建，整合长时程、跨场景和异质行为模式。基于该基准，首先提供实证证据，然后对最先进的LLM进行广泛评估，并系统比较模拟行为与真实行为。

Result: 1. 先前孤立场景数据集存在"隧道视野"问题，而真实世界决策依赖于长时程、跨场景的因果链。2. 当前LLM难以准确模拟这些复杂行为，即使上下文窗口扩大，性能也会达到平台期。3. 发现LLM存在结构性偏见：倾向于收敛到"积极平均人"，表现出过度活跃、角色同质化和乌托邦偏见，导致个体差异和长尾行为丢失。

Conclusion: OmniBehavior基准揭示了当前LLM在用户模拟中的根本局限性，特别是结构性偏见问题。这为未来高保真模拟研究指明了关键方向，需要解决个体差异和长尾行为的保留问题。

Abstract: The emergence of Large Language Models (LLMs) has illuminated the potential for a general-purpose user simulator. However, existing benchmarks remain constrained to isolated scenarios, narrow action spaces, or synthetic data, failing to capture the holistic nature of authentic human behavior. To bridge this gap, we introduce OmniBehavior, the first user simulation benchmark constructed entirely from real-world data, integrating long-horizon, cross-scenario, and heterogeneous behavioral patterns into a unified framework. Based on this benchmark, we first provide empirical evidence that previous datasets with isolated scenarios suffer from tunnel vision, whereas real-world decision-making relies on long-term, cross-scenario causal chains. Extensive evaluations of state-of-the-art LLMs reveal that current models struggle to accurately simulate these complex behaviors, with performance plateauing even as context windows expand. Crucially, a systematic comparison between simulated and authentic behaviors uncovers a fundamental structural bias: LLMs tend to converge toward a positive average person, exhibiting hyper-activity, persona homogenization, and a Utopian bias. This results in the loss of individual differences and long-tail behaviors, highlighting critical directions for future high-fidelity simulation research.

</details>


### [50] [A GAN and LLM-Driven Data Augmentation Framework for Dynamic Linguistic Pattern Modeling in Chinese Sarcasm Detection](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.08381)
*Wenxian Wang,Xiaohu Luo,Junfeng Hao,Xiaoming Gu,Xingshu Chen,Zhu Wang,Haizhou Wang*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 提出基于GAN和LLM的数据增强框架，结合用户历史行为建模，用于中文反讽检测，在微博数据集上取得SOTA性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有中文反讽检测方法受限于数据集规模小、构建成本高，且主要关注文本特征，忽略了用户特有的语言模式对观点和情感表达的影响。

Method: 1) 从微博多话题收集原始数据；2) 训练GAN并应用GPT-3.5数据增强技术合成扩展的反讽评论数据集SinaSarc；3) 扩展BERT架构以融入多维信息（特别是用户历史行为），捕捉动态语言模式。

Result: 模型在非反讽和反讽类别上分别获得0.9138和0.9151的F1分数，优于所有现有SOTA方法。

Conclusion: 提出了一个新颖的框架，用于在中文反讽检测中动态建模用户长期语言模式，为该领域的数据集构建和方法论进步做出贡献。

Abstract: Sarcasm is a rhetorical device that expresses criticism or emphasizes characteristics of certain individuals or situations through exaggeration, irony, or comparison. Existing methods for Chinese sarcasm detection are constrained by limited datasets and high construction costs, and they mainly focus on textual features, overlooking user-specific linguistic patterns that shape how opinions and emotions are expressed. This paper proposes a Generative Adversarial Network (GAN) and Large Language Model (LLM)-driven data augmentation framework to dynamically model users' linguistic patterns for enhanced Chinese sarcasm detection. First, we collect raw data from various topics on Sina Weibo. Then, we train a GAN on these data and apply a GPT-3.5 based data augmentation technique to synthesize an extended sarcastic comment dataset, named SinaSarc. This dataset contains target comments, contextual information, and user historical behavior. Finally, we extend the BERT architecture to incorporate multi-dimensional information, particularly user historical behavior, enabling the model to capture dynamic linguistic patterns and uncover implicit sarcastic cues in comments. Experimental results demonstrate the effectiveness of our proposed method. Specifically, our model achieves the highest F1-scores on both the non-sarcastic and sarcastic categories, with values of 0.9138 and 0.9151 respectively, which outperforms all existing state-of-the-art (SOTA) approaches. This study presents a novel framework for dynamically modeling users' long-term linguistic patterns in Chinese sarcasm detection, contributing to both dataset construction and methodological advancement in this field.

</details>


### [51] [Synthetic Data for any Differentiable Target](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.08423)
*Tristan Thrush,Sung Min Park,Herman Brunborg,Luke Bailey,Marcel Roed,Neil Band,Christopher Potts,Tatsunori Hashimoto*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: DPG是一种通过强化学习优化合成数据生成器的方法，能够精确生成目标训练数据，仅通过监督微调就能控制目标模型的行为和参数。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 探索通过合成训练数据控制语言模型的极限，开发一种能够精确优化合成数据生成器的方法，使其产生针对特定目标的训练数据集。

Method: 提出数据集策略梯度（DPG）强化学习原语，通过高阶梯度进行精确数据归因，将这些分数作为策略梯度奖励，近似合成数据生成器的真实梯度。

Result: 仅通过SFT就能实现：1）在LM头权重中嵌入QR码；2）嵌入模式"67"；3）降低ℓ²范数；4）将输入重述为新语言；5）生成特定UUID，即使这些目标未在生成器输入提示中体现。

Conclusion: DPG是一种强大而灵活的技术，仅使用合成训练示例就能塑造模型属性，展示了通过合成数据精确控制语言模型的潜力。

Abstract: What are the limits of controlling language models via synthetic training data? We develop a reinforcement learning (RL) primitive, the Dataset Policy Gradient (DPG), which can precisely optimize synthetic data generators to produce a dataset of targeted examples. When used for supervised fine-tuning (SFT) of a target model, these examples cause the target model to do well on a differentiable metric of our choice. Our approach achieves this by taking exact data attribution via higher-order gradients and using those scores as policy gradient rewards. We prove that this procedure closely approximates the true, intractable gradient for the synthetic data generator. To illustrate the potential of DPG, we show that, using only SFT on generated examples, we can cause the target model's LM head weights to (1) embed a QR code, (2) embed the pattern $\texttt{67}$, and (3) have lower $\ell^2$ norm. We additionally show that we can cause the generator to (4) rephrase inputs in a new language and (5) produce a specific UUID, even though neither of these objectives is conveyed in the generator's input prompts. These findings suggest that DPG is a powerful and flexible technique for shaping model properties using only synthetic training examples.

</details>


### [52] [AfriVoices-KE: A Multilingual Speech Dataset for Kenyan Languages](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.08448)
*Lilian Wanzare,Cynthia Amol,zekiel Maina,Nelson Odhiambo,Hope Kerubo,Leila Misula,Vivian Oloo,Rennish Mboya,Edwin Onkoba,Edward Ombui,Joseph Muguro,Ciira wa Maina,Andrew Kipkebut,Alfred Omondi Otom,Ian Ndung'u Kang'ethe,Angela Wambui Kanyi,Brian Gichana Omwenga*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: AfriVoices-KE是一个包含约3000小时音频的大规模多语言语音数据集，涵盖肯尼亚五种语言，旨在解决非洲语言在语音技术中的代表性不足问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 解决非洲语言在语音技术中的关键代表性不足问题，为肯尼亚的多种语言提供高质量、语言多样性的资源，促进包容性语音技术的发展并推进肯尼亚语言遗产的数字保存。

Method: 采用双重方法收集数据：脚本语音从编译的文本语料库、翻译和领域特定生成的句子中获取，涵盖肯尼亚背景下的11个领域；非脚本语音通过文本和图像提示引出以捕捉自然语言变化和方言细微差别。使用定制的移动应用程序让贡献者通过智能手机录音。质量保证采用多层方法，包括录制前的自动信噪比验证和人工内容准确性审查。

Result: 创建了包含约3000小时音频的大规模多语言语音数据集，涵盖Dholuo、Kikuyu、Kalenjin、Maasai和Somali五种肯尼亚语言，包括750小时脚本语音和2250小时自发语音，收集自4777名母语者，覆盖不同地区和人口统计群体。

Conclusion: AfriVoices-KE为开发包容性自动语音识别和文本转语音系统提供了基础资源，同时推进了肯尼亚语言遗产的数字保存。尽管在低资源环境中面临基础设施不可靠、设备兼容性问题和社区信任障碍等挑战，但通过当地动员者、利益相关者伙伴关系和适应性培训协议得到了缓解。

Abstract: AfriVoices-KE is a large-scale multilingual speech dataset comprising approximately 3,000 hours of audio across five Kenyan languages: Dholuo, Kikuyu, Kalenjin, Maasai, and Somali. The dataset includes 750 hours of scripted speech and 2,250 hours of spontaneous speech, collected from 4,777 native speakers across diverse regions and demographics. This work addresses the critical underrepresentation of African languages in speech technology by providing a high-quality, linguistically diverse resource. Data collection followed a dual methodology: scripted recordings drew from compiled text corpora, translations, and domain-specific generated sentences spanning eleven domains relevant to the Kenyan context, while unscripted speech was elicited through textual and image prompts to capture natural linguistic variation and dialectal nuances. A customized mobile application enabled contributors to record using smartphones. Quality assurance operated at multiple layers, encompassing automated signal-to-noise ratio validation prior to recording and human review for content accuracy. Though the project encountered challenges common to low-resource settings, including unreliable infrastructure, device compatibility issues, and community trust barriers, these were mitigated through local mobilizers, stakeholder partnerships, and adaptive training protocols. AfriVoices-KE provides a foundational resource for developing inclusive automatic speech recognition and text-to-speech systems, while advancing the digital preservation of Kenya's linguistic heritage.

</details>


### [53] [AI generates well-liked but templatic empathic responses](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.08479)
*Emma Gueorguieva,Hongli Zhan,Jina Suh,Javier Hernandez,Tatiana Lau,Junyi Jessy Li,Desmond C. Ong*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 研究发现LLM在表达同理心时高度公式化，使用固定的策略模板，而人类回应则更加多样化


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 研究动机是理解为什么人们认为LLM的同理心回应比人类回应更具同理心，探索LLM表达同理心的语言模式

Method: 开发了包含10种同理心语言策略的分类法，应用于分析AI生成和人类撰写的同理心回应。通过两项研究比较了3,265个AI生成回应（来自6个模型）和1,290个人类撰写回应

Result: 发现LLM回应在话语功能层面高度公式化，识别出一个策略模板，匹配83-90%的LLM回应（在保留样本中为60-83%），且匹配时覆盖81-92%的回应内容。人类回应则更加多样化

Conclusion: LLM通过学习和一致应用受欢迎的同理心表达模板获得成功，这对AI生成同理心的未来发展具有重要启示

Abstract: Recent research shows that greater numbers of people are turning to Large Language Models (LLMs) for emotional support, and that people rate LLM responses as more empathic than human-written responses. We suggest a reason for this success: LLMs have learned and consistently deploy a well-liked template for expressing empathy. We develop a taxonomy of 10 empathic language "tactics" that include validating someone's feelings and paraphrasing, and apply this taxonomy to characterize the language that people and LLMs produce when writing empathic responses. Across a set of 2 studies comparing a total of n = 3,265 AI-generated (by six models) and n = 1,290 human-written responses, we find that LLM responses are highly formulaic at a discourse functional level. We discovered a template -- a structured sequence of tactics -- that matches between 83--90% of LLM responses (and 60--83\% in a held out sample), and when those are matched, covers 81--92% of the response. By contrast, human-written responses are more diverse. We end with a discussion of implications for the future of AI-generated empathy.

</details>


### [54] [What do Language Models Learn and When? The Implicit Curriculum Hypothesis](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.08510)
*Emmy Liu,Kaiser Sun,Millicent Li,Isabelle Lee,Lindia Tjuatja,Jen-tse Huang,Graham Neubig*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 该论文提出"隐性课程假说"，认为预训练遵循跨模型和数据混合的组合性、可预测的课程，并通过设计简单可组合任务套件验证了这一假说。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 尽管大语言模型能执行复杂任务，但预训练过程中能力如何具体涌现的细粒度细节仍不清楚。验证损失的缩放定律告诉我们模型如何随计算量提升而改进，但无法揭示模型在何时获得何种技能。

Method: 设计了一套涵盖检索、形态变换、共指、逻辑推理和数学的简单可组合任务套件，在四个模型家族（410M-13B参数）上追踪涌现点，分析任务表示空间与训练轨迹的关系。

Result: 发现模型达到固定准确度阈值的涌现顺序高度一致（45个模型对的ρ=0.81），复合任务通常在组件任务之后涌现；任务表示相似性与其训练轨迹相似性相关；基于任务表示空间可有效预测未见过组合任务的训练轨迹（R²=0.68-0.84）。

Conclusion: 预训练比损失曲线显示的结构化程度更高：技能以组合性顺序涌现，这种顺序在不同模型间一致且可从模型内部表示中解读。

Abstract: Large language models (LLMs) can perform remarkably complex tasks, yet the fine-grained details of how these capabilities emerge during pretraining remain poorly understood. Scaling laws on validation loss tell us how much a model improves with additional compute, but not what skills it acquires in which order. To remedy this, we propose the Implicit Curriculum Hypothesis: pretraining follows a compositional and predictable curriculum across models and data mixtures. We test this by designing a suite of simple, composable tasks spanning retrieval, morphological transformations, coreference, logical reasoning, and mathematics. Using these tasks, we track emergence points across four model families spanning sizes from 410M-13B parameters. We find that emergence orderings of when models reach fixed accuracy thresholds are strikingly consistent ($ρ= .81$ across 45 model pairs), and that composite tasks most often emerge after their component tasks. Furthermore, we find that this structure is encoded in model representations: tasks with similar function vector representations also tend to follow similar trajectories in training. By using the space of representations derived from our task set, we can effectively predict the training trajectories of simple held-out compositional tasks throughout the course of pretraining ($R^2 = .68$-$.84$ across models) without previously evaluating them. Together, these results suggest that pretraining is more structured than loss curves reveal: skills emerge in a compositional order that is consistent across models and readable from their internals.

</details>


### [55] [Cram Less to Fit More: Training Data Pruning Improves Memorization of Facts](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.08519)
*Jiayuan Ye,Vitaly Feldman,Kunal Talwar*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 该研究从信息论角度形式化事实记忆问题，提出基于训练损失的数据选择方法，通过限制训练数据中事实数量并平铺其频率分布，显著提升语言模型的事实记忆能力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 大型语言模型在参数中记忆事实知识存在困难，常导致幻觉和知识密集型任务表现不佳。研究旨在从信息论角度理解事实记忆，并探索训练数据分布如何影响事实准确性。

Method: 1. 从信息论角度形式化事实记忆问题；2. 提出基于训练损失的数据选择方案，限制训练数据中的事实数量并平铺其频率分布（特别是针对幂律分布）；3. 在半合成数据集和带标注的维基百科语料库上进行实验验证。

Result: 1. 在半合成数据集上，选择方法能将事实准确性提升至容量极限；2. 在维基百科预训练中，GPT2-Small模型（1.1亿参数）能记忆比标准训练多1.3倍的实体事实，性能匹配在完整数据集上预训练的10倍大模型（13亿参数）。

Conclusion: 通过信息论视角分析事实记忆问题，并设计基于训练损失的数据选择策略，能有效提升语言模型的事实记忆能力，使小模型达到与大模型相当的事实记忆性能，为高效知识记忆提供了新思路。

Abstract: Large language models (LLMs) can struggle to memorize factual knowledge in their parameters, often leading to hallucinations and poor performance on knowledge-intensive tasks. In this paper, we formalize fact memorization from an information-theoretic perspective and study how training data distributions affect fact accuracy. We show that fact accuracy is suboptimal (below the capacity limit) whenever the amount of information contained in the training data facts exceeds model capacity. This is further exacerbated when the fact frequency distribution is skewed (e.g. a power law).
  We propose data selection schemes based on the training loss alone that aim to limit the number of facts in the training data and flatten their frequency distribution. On semi-synthetic datasets containing high-entropy facts, our selection method effectively boosts fact accuracy to the capacity limit. When pretraining language models from scratch on an annotated Wikipedia corpus, our selection method enables a GPT2-Small model (110m parameters) to memorize 1.3X more entity facts compared to standard training, matching the performance of a 10X larger model (1.3B parameters) pretrained on the full dataset.

</details>


### [56] [Demystifying OPD: Length Inflation and Stabilization Strategies for Large Language Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.08527)
*Feng Luo,Yu-Neng Chuang,Guanchu Wang,Zicheng Xu,Xiaotian Han,Tianyi Zhang,Vladimir Braverman*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: OPD存在截断崩溃问题：学生模型诱导的数据收集与蒸馏目标交互导致生成长度急剧膨胀和重复饱和，引发训练不稳定和性能下降。作者提出StableOPD框架，通过参考分布约束和混合蒸馏来稳定训练。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 发现on-policy蒸馏(OPD)存在一个失败模式：随着训练进行，on-policy rollout会经历急剧的长度膨胀，导致截断轨迹主导训练数据。这种截断崩溃与重复饱和同时发生，引发有偏梯度信号，导致严重的训练不稳定和验证性能急剧下降。

Method: 提出StableOPD框架，结合两个关键组件：1) 基于参考分布的散度约束，防止学生分布过度偏离参考分布；2) rollout混合蒸馏，混合不同来源的rollout数据来缓解重复诱导的长度膨胀问题。

Result: 在多个数学推理数据集上的实验表明，该方法有效防止了截断崩溃，稳定了训练动态，平均性能提升了7.2%。

Conclusion: OPD中的截断崩溃问题源于学生诱导的数据收集与蒸馏目标的交互作用，StableOPD通过参考分布约束和混合蒸馏有效解决了这一问题，显著提升了训练稳定性和模型性能。

Abstract: On-policy distillation (OPD) trains student models under their own induced distribution while leveraging supervision from stronger teachers. We identify a failure mode of OPD: as training progresses, on-policy rollouts can undergo abrupt length inflation, causing truncated trajectories to dominate the training data. This truncation collapse coincides with abrupt repetition saturation and induces biased gradient signals, leading to severe training instability and sharp degradation in validation performance. We attribute this problem to the interaction between student-induced data collection and the distillation objective, which implicitly favors long and repetitive rollouts. To address this issue, we propose StableOPD, a stabilized OPD framework that combines a reference-based divergence constraint with rollout mixture distillation. These together mitigate repetition-induced length inflation and further stabilize OPD training. Across multiple math reasoning datasets, our approach prevents truncation collapse, stabilizes training dynamics, and improves performance by 7.2% on average.

</details>

### [57] [Linearly Solvable Continuous-Time General-Sum Stochastic Differential Games](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.07479)
*Monika Tomar,Takashi Tanaka*

Main category: math.OC

TL;DR: 该论文提出了一类连续时间有限玩家随机一般和微分博弈，通过精确的线性PDE系统求解，利用交叉对数似然比建模多智能体空间冲突，通过广义多元Cole-Hopf变换将非线性HJB方程解耦为线性PDE系统，实现无网格计算反馈纳什均衡策略。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统多智能体随机微分博弈面临维度诅咒和非线性HJB方程求解困难的问题，特别是在处理空间冲突（如拥堵避免）等实际应用场景时，需要开发高效的计算方法。

Method: 提出基于交叉对数似然比的分布规划博弈框架，应用广义多元Cole-Hopf变换将非线性HJB方程解耦为线性PDE系统，利用Feynman-Kac路径积分方法实现无网格计算反馈纳什均衡策略。

Result: 成功将复杂的非线性HJB方程转化为线性PDE系统，有效克服了维度诅咒问题，实现了多智能体随机微分博弈反馈纳什均衡策略的高效计算。

Conclusion: 该方法为连续时间有限玩家随机一般和微分博弈提供了一种有效的解析和计算框架，特别适用于多智能体空间冲突建模，具有重要的理论和应用价值。

Abstract: This paper introduces a class of continuous-time, finite-player stochastic general-sum differential games that admit solutions through an exact linear PDE system. We formulate a distribution planning game utilizing the cross-log-likelihood ratio to naturally model multi-agent spatial conflicts, such as congestion avoidance. By applying a generalized multivariate Cole-Hopf transformation, we decouple the associated non-linear Hamilton-Jacobi-Bellman (HJB) equations into a system of linear partial differential equations. This reduction enables the efficient, grid-free computation of feedback Nash equilibrium strategies via the Feynman-Kac path integral method, effectively overcoming the curse of dimensionality.

</details>


### [58] [Parameter-free non-ergodic extragradient algorithms for solving monotone variational inequalities](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.07662)
*Lingqing Shen,Fatma Kılınç-Karzan*

Main category: math.OC

TL;DR: 提出参数无关的外梯度方法，用于单调变分不等式问题，具有非渐近的最后迭代保证，适用于全局和局部Lipschitz算子


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有外梯度方法的性能严重依赖于步长选择，且大多数理论关注迭代的遍历平均，而实际性能往往由最后迭代决定。现有最后迭代保证通常需要基于问题特定全局光滑信息的固定步长，这种信息难以准确估计且可能不适用。

Method: 开发参数无关的外梯度方法：1) 对于全局Lipschitz算子，算法达到o(1/√T)的最后迭代收敛率；2) 通过回溯线搜索扩展到局部Lipschitz算子，保持参数无关性，获得相同收敛率。

Result: 在双线性矩阵博弈、LASSO、极小极大群体公平和最先进的最大熵采样松弛等实验中，展示了方法的广泛适用性、强大的最后迭代性能以及对现有方法的显著改进。

Conclusion: 提出的参数无关外梯度方法为约束单调变分不等式提供了非渐近的最后迭代保证，使参数无关的最后迭代方法适用于全局光滑性不现实的广泛问题类别。

Abstract: Monotone variational inequalities (VIs) provide a unifying framework for convex minimization, equilibrium computation, and convex-concave saddle-point problems. Extragradient-type methods are among the most effective first-order algorithms for such problems, but their performance hinges critically on stepsize selection. While most existing theory focuses on ergodic averages of the iterates, practical performance is often driven by the significantly stronger behavior of the last iterate. Moreover, available last-iterate guarantees typically rely on fixed stepsizes chosen using problem-specific global smoothness information, which is often difficult to estimate accurately and may not even be applicable. In this paper, we develop parameter-free extragradient methods with non-asymptotic last-iterate guarantees for constrained monotone VIs. For globally Lipschitz operators, our algorithm achieves an $o(1/\sqrt{T})$ last-iterate rate. We then extend the framework to locally Lipschitz operators via backtracking line search and obtain the same rate while preserving parameter-freeness, thereby making parameter-free last-iterate methods applicable to important problem classes for which global smoothness is unrealistic. Our numerical experiments on bilinear matrix games, LASSO, minimax group fairness, and state-of-the-art maximum entropy sampling relaxations demonstrate wide applicability of our results as well as strong last-iterate performance and significant improvements over existing methods.

</details>


### [59] [On Linear Critical-Region Boundaries in Continuous-Time Multiparametric Optimal Control](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.07693)
*Lida Lamakani,Efstratios N. Pistikopoulos*

Main category: math.OC

TL;DR: 论文研究了连续时间最优控制问题的显式控制方法，证明了临界区域边界为超平面的充要条件是相关极值点出现在初始或终端时刻，而与初始条件无关。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 研究连续时间多参数显式控制中临界区域边界的几何特性，特别是何时边界为超平面而非曲面，这对构建分区有直接影响。

Method: 通过分析拉格朗日乘子和约束函数沿最优轨迹的极值点，证明边界为超平面的充要条件是极值点出现在固定端点（初始或终端时刻）。利用共态变量是初始状态线性函数的特点，推导边界法向量可通过矩阵指数和线性求解计算。

Result: 边界为超平面当且仅当相关极值点出现在初始或终端时刻；若极值点随时间变化（如切换时间或内部驻点），边界通常为曲面。在三阶系统上首次解析获得完整三维临界区域分区，并与离散时间公式对比。

Conclusion: 连续时间多参数显式控制中，临界区域边界的几何特性由极值点出现的时间决定：固定端点产生超平面边界，时变极值点产生曲面边界。这一发现简化了分区构建，且连续时间分区在离散化时保持不变，而离散时间分区复杂度急剧增加。

Abstract: When an optimal control problem is solved for all possible initial conditions at once, the initial-state space splits into critical regions, each carrying a closed-form control law that can be evaluated online without solving any optimization. This is the multiparametric approach to explicit control. In the continuous-time setting, the boundaries between these regions are determined by extrema of Lagrange multipliers and constraint functions along the optimal trajectory. Whether a boundary is a hyperplane, computable analytically, or a curved manifold that requires numerical methods has a direct effect on how the partition is built.
  We show that a boundary is a hyperplane if and only if the relevant extremum is attained at either the initial time or the terminal time, regardless of the initial condition. The reason is that the costate is a linear function of the initial state at any fixed time, so when the extremum is tied to a fixed endpoint, the boundary condition is linear and the boundary normal follows directly from two matrix exponentials and a linear solve. When the extremum occurs at a time that shifts with the initial condition, such as a switching time or an interior stationary point, the boundary is generally curved.
  We demonstrate the result on a third-order system, obtaining the complete three-dimensional critical-region partition analytically for the first time in this problem class. A comparison with a discrete-time formulation shows how sharply the region count grows under discretization, while the continuous-time partition remains unchanged.

</details>


### [60] [Optimizing Treatment Allocation to Maximize the Health of a Population](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.07738)
*Daniel Adelman,Alba V Olivares-Nadal,Miaolan Xie*

Main category: math.OC

TL;DR: 提出基于Measurized MDPs的PHM新方法，通过有限患者集优化长期人口分布，生成基于调整影响度的简单索引策略，在CMS数据上显著优于短视基准


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: PHM优化面临高维患者协变量管理、长期干预响应跟踪、人口流动等挑战，需要解决稀缺治疗资源分配问题，同时确保伦理合规性

Method: 采用Measurized MDPs框架，建立优化长期医疗人口分布的MDP模型，引入非伤害性约束限制死亡率，使用ADP将无限维问题简化为识别有限的高性能治疗/未治疗患者集

Result: 生成简单临床可实施的索引策略：当患者调整影响度超过阈值时选择治疗。在CMS数据上显示统计显著优于短视基准，优势随时间范围增加，最长测试范围对应每年每1000名患者增加1500多个家庭日

Conclusion: 提出的Measurized MDPs方法有效解决PHM优化挑战，生成简单实用的临床策略，在长期人口健康管理中具有显著优势，同时确保伦理合规性

Abstract: Recent shifts in global health priorities have positioned Population Health Management (PHM) as a central area of focus. However, optimizing PHM strategies presents several challenges: managing high-dimensional patient covariates, tracking their evolution and long-term response to interventions, and accounting for the inflow and outflow of individuals within the population. In this paper, we propose a novel approach based on Measurized MDPs that integrates these components. We consider a setting in which a treatment with population-level benefits is available but scarce, and model an MDP that optimizes the long-term distribution of the healthcare population under expected capacity constraints. This formulation allows us to bypass both the dimensionality and practical challenges of handling and tracking individual patient covariates across the population. To ensure ethical compliance, we introduce a non-maleficence constraint that limits the allowable mortality rate. To solve the resulting infinite-dimensional problem, we use ADP and reduce the task to identifying a finite set of high-performing treated and untreated patients. Despite the complexity of the underlying structure, our approach yields a simple, clinically implementable index policy: a patient is selected for treatment if their adjusted impactability exceeds a specified threshold. The adjusted impactability captures the long-term consequences of receiving or not receiving treatment. While straightforward to apply, the policy remains flexible and can incorporate general machine learning models. Using CMS data, we show that our policy yields a statistically significant improvement over a myopic benchmark. This advantage increases with the time horizon, consistent with the forward-looking nature of our policy. At the longest horizon tested, this corresponds to over 1,500 additional home days annually per 1,000 patients.

</details>


### [61] [Unified Precision-Guaranteed Stopping Rules for Contextual Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.07913)
*Mingrui Ding,Qiuhong Zhao,Siyang Gao,Jing Dong*

Main category: math.OC

TL;DR: 开发了基于广义似然比统计量的统一停止规则，用于上下文学习中的数据收集停止决策，在未知抽样方差下保证学习策略的精度，显著减少所需样本量。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在运营管理中，上下文学习需要从各种数据源收集数据来确定决策策略。核心问题是如何确定何时可以停止数据收集，同时保证学习到的策略足够准确。现有方法在未知抽样方差情况下缺乏有效的停止规则，且传统方法因分离均值和方差不确定性而过于保守。

Method: 提出基于广义似然比（GLR）统计量的统一停止规则，用于非结构化和结构化线性设置中的上下文学习。开发新的时间一致偏差不等式来校准顺序边界，直接控制自归一化的GLR证据，避免均值和方差不确定性分离导致的保守性。在Gaussian抽样模型下建立有限样本精度保证。

Result: 在合成实例和两个案例研究中，所提出的停止规则相比基准方法，以显著更少的样本量达到目标精度。框架适用于多种数据收集环境（历史数据集、仿真模型、实际系统），能够在保持决策质量的同时减少不必要的抽样。

Conclusion: 该框架为个性化决策问题提供了确定何时收集足够信息的实用方法，通过直接控制GLR证据避免传统方法的保守性，在多种数据环境中都能有效减少样本需求，同时保证决策策略的精度要求。

Abstract: Contextual learning seeks to learn a decision policy that maps an individual's characteristics to an action through data collection. In operations management, such data may come from various sources, and a central question is when data collection can stop while still guaranteeing that the learned policy is sufficiently accurate. We study this question under two precision criteria: a context-wise criterion and an aggregate policy-value criterion. We develop unified stopping rules for contextual learning with unknown sampling variances in both unstructured and structured linear settings. Our approach is based on generalized likelihood ratio (GLR) statistics for pairwise action comparisons. To calibrate the corresponding sequential boundaries, we derive new time-uniform deviation inequalities that directly control the self-normalized GLR evidence and thus avoid the conservativeness caused by decoupling mean and variance uncertainty. Under the Gaussian sampling model, we establish finite-sample precision guarantees for both criteria. Numerical experiments on synthetic instances and two case studies demonstrate that the proposed stopping rules achieve the target precision with substantially fewer samples than benchmark methods. The proposed framework provides a practical way to determine when enough information has been collected in personalized decision problems. It applies across multiple data-collection environments, including historical datasets, simulation models, and real systems, enabling practitioners to reduce unnecessary sampling while maintaining a desired level of decision quality.

</details>


### [62] [Smooth, globally Polyak-Łojasiewicz functions are nonlinear least-squares](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.07972)
*Nicolas Boumal,Christopher Criscitiello,Quentin Rebjock*

Main category: math.OC

TL;DR: 该论文证明了在黎曼流形上，光滑的Polyak-Łojasiewicz条件函数具有严格的全局结构：必须是平方和形式，其极小值集要么是欧几里得空间（函数可转化为凸二次型），要么具有奇异几何结构。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 研究Polyak-Łojasiewicz条件在非凸优化中虽然能保证快速收敛，但这一逐点的一阶不等式对函数及其极小值集施加了何种全局结构约束尚不清楚。作者旨在揭示PŁ条件在光滑黎曼流形上的深层几何含义。

Method: 通过证明负梯度流的端点映射是平凡光滑纤维丛，建立了PŁ函数必须具有平方和形式的结构定理。利用这一刚性结果，分析了极小值集的几何分类：要么是欧几里得空间，要么是奇异流形。

Result: 1. 光滑PŁ函数必为平方和形式：f(x)=f*+‖φ(x)‖²，其中φ是浸没映射；2. 极小值集S要么微分同胚于欧几里得空间（此时f可通过坐标变换化为凸二次型），要么具有奇异几何（如Whitehead流形）；3. 存在完备黎曼度量使f保持PŁ且成为测地凸函数。

Conclusion: Polyak-Łojasiewicz条件在光滑黎曼流形上施加了极强的几何约束：函数必须是平方和形式，极小值集具有严格的二分结构。这一发现揭示了PŁ条件在非凸优化中的深层几何本质，并为理解优化问题的几何结构提供了新视角。

Abstract: The Polyak-Łojasiewicz (PŁ) condition is often invoked in nonconvex optimization because it allows fast convergence of algorithms beyond strong convexity. A function $f \colon \mathcal{M} \to \mathbb{R}$ on a Riemannian manifold $\mathcal{M}$ is globally PŁ if $\|\nabla f(x)\|^2 \geq 2μ(f(x) - f^*)$ for all $x$, where $f^* = \inf f$ and $μ> 0$. How much does this pointwise, first-order inequality constrain $f$ and its set of minimizers $S$?
  We show that if $f$ is also smooth ($C^\infty$) and $\mathcal{M}$ is contractible (e.g., if $\mathcal{M} = \mathbb{R}^n$), then the PŁ condition imposes a firm global structure: such a function is necessarily of the form $f(x) = f^* + \|\varphi(x)\|^2$ (a nonlinear sum of squares) where $\varphi \colon \mathcal{M} \to \mathbb{R}^k$ is a submersion, and $k$ is the codimension of $S$ in $\mathcal{M}$. The proof hinges on showing that the end-point map of negative gradient flow on $f$ is a trivial smooth fiber bundle over $S$.
  This rigidity leads to a striking dichotomy. Either $S$ is diffeomorphic to a Euclidean space, in which case $f$ can be transformed into a convex quadratic by a smooth change of coordinates. Or $S$ must display genuinely exotic geometry; for example, it can be diffeomorphic to the Whitehead manifold.
  As a further consequence, we show that there exists a complete Riemannian metric on $\mathcal{M}$ under which $f$ remains PŁ and becomes geodesically convex.

</details>


### [63] [Inexact Limited Memory Bundle Method](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.08067)
*Jenni Lampainen,Kaisa Joki,Napsu Karmitsa,Marko M. Mäkelä*

Main category: math.OC

TL;DR: 提出了一种用于大规模非光滑非凸优化的新型不精确有限内存束方法，该方法能容忍函数值和次梯度的噪声，并证明了其全局收敛性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 大规模非光滑优化问题在实际应用中广泛存在，但获取精确的函数值和次梯度值可能计算成本高昂甚至不可行。在许多实际场景中，由于测量误差、建模误差、隐私保护计算或随机近似等原因，只能获得不精确信息，因此需要开发能处理不精确信息的优化方法。

Method: 提出了一种新型的不精确有限内存束方法，专门针对大规模非光滑非凸优化问题设计。该方法能够容忍函数值和次梯度中的噪声，采用有限内存束技术来处理大规模问题，并包含不精确信息处理机制。

Result: 证明了所提方法的全局收敛性，能够收敛到近似驻点。通过在不同噪声水平下（函数值和/或次梯度值）的数值实验表明，该方法在精确数据和噪声数据下都表现良好。结果展示了该方法在大规模非光滑优化中的竞争力，特别适用于噪声不可避免的应用场景，如机器学习中的差分隐私。

Conclusion: 该研究提出了一种有效处理不精确信息的大规模非光滑非凸优化方法，具有理论保证和实际应用价值，特别适用于存在噪声的现实世界优化问题。

Abstract: Large-scale nonsmooth optimization problems arise in many real-world applications, but obtaining exact function and subgradient values for these problems may be computationally expensive or even infeasible. In many practical settings, only inexact information is available due to measurement or modeling errors, privacy-preserving computations, or stochastic approximations, making inexact optimization methods particularly relevant. In this paper, we propose a novel inexact limited memory bundle method for large-scale nonsmooth nonconvex optimization. The method tolerates noise in both function values and subgradients. We prove the global convergence of the proposed method to an approximate stationary point. Numerical experiments with different levels of noise in function and/or subgradient values show that the method performs well with both exact and noisy data. In particular, the results demonstrate competitiveness in large-scale nonsmooth optimization and highlight the suitability of the method for applications where noise is unavoidable, such as differential privacy in machine learning.

</details>


### [64] [Dual Approaches to Stochastic Control via SPDEs and the Pathwise Hopf Formula](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.08155)
*Mathieu Laurière,Jiefei Yang*

Main category: math.OC

TL;DR: 提出连续时间随机控制问题的对偶方法，基于Rogers的对偶公式，将内层优化问题表述为随机偏微分方程，其解的期望给出对偶界，并基于Pontryagin最大值原理和广义Hopf公式提出维度诅咒自由方法，证明了广义Hopf公式。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 解决高维状态和控制空间中的连续时间随机控制问题，传统方法面临维度诅咒，需要开发能够有效计算鲁棒对偶界的方法来补充原始方法。

Method: 基于Rogers的对偶公式，将内层优化问题表述为随机偏微分方程，利用Pontryagin最大值原理和广义Hopf公式构建维度诅咒自由方法，并证明了广义Hopf公式在温和条件下的正确性。

Result: 提出的对偶方法能够有效补充原始方法，包括用于求解高维PDE的深度BSDE方法和强化学习中的深度演员-评论家方法，数值实验验证了其有效性。

Conclusion: 开发的对偶方法为高维连续时间随机控制问题提供了有效的计算工具，能够计算鲁棒对偶界，补充现有原始方法的不足，具有理论和实用价值。

Abstract: We develop dual approaches for continuous-time stochastic control problems, enabling the computation of robust dual bounds in high-dimensional state and control spaces. Building on the dual formulation proposed in [L. C. G. Rogers, SIAM Journal on Control and Optimization, 46 (2007), pp. 1116--1132], we first formulate the inner optimization problem as a stochastic partial differential equation (SPDE); the expectation of its solution yields the dual bound. Curse-of-dimensionality-free methods are proposed based on the Pontryagin maximum principle and the generalized Hopf formula. In the process, we prove the generalized Hopf formula, first introduced as a conjecture in [Y. T. Chow, J. Darbon, S. Osher, and W. Yin, Journal of Computational Physics 387 (2019), pp. 376--409], under mild conditions. Numerical experiments demonstrate that our dual approaches effectively complement primal methods, including the deep BSDE method for solving high-dimensional PDEs and the deep actor-critic method in reinforcement learning.

</details>


### [65] [Free-Energy Minimizing Policies Under Generative Model Ambiguity](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.08222)
*Arash Shafiei,Caio César Graciani Rodrigues,Giovanni Russo*

Main category: math.OC

TL;DR: 提出一种基于变分自由能的分布鲁棒决策框架，用于处理生成模型中的模糊性，通过求解一个极小极大最优控制问题来获得鲁棒策略。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在决策过程中，生成模型通常存在模糊性和不确定性，传统方法可能对模型误差敏感。需要一种分布鲁棒的方法来处理这种模型不确定性，确保决策在模糊集内保持鲁棒性。

Method: 基于变分自由能原理，将分布鲁棒决策问题形式化为一个极小极大最优控制问题，其中最大化部分在表示模糊性的不确定性集合上进行。证明了最优策略需要求解非凸最小化问题，并提出具有收敛保证的算法。

Result: 提出的算法具有收敛保证，能够找到最优策略。通过摆锤摆起问题的仿真验证了方法的有效性，展示了在模型不确定性下的鲁棒性能。

Conclusion: 变分自由能框架为处理生成模型模糊性的分布鲁棒决策提供了一种有效方法，将问题转化为极小极大最优控制问题，并开发了具有收敛保证的求解算法，在控制任务中表现出良好的鲁棒性。

Abstract: We present a variational free-energy formulation for distributionally robust decision-making with ambiguity in the generative model. The formulation, related to a broad range of learning and control frameworks, yields a minimax optimal control problem where maximization is over an uncertainty set that represents ambiguities. We prove that computing the optimal policy requires solving a non-convex minimization problem and propose an algorithm with convergence guarantees to find the solution. The effectiveness of our results is illustrated via simulations on a pendulum swing-up problem.

</details>


### [66] [Finite-time Reachability for Constrained, Partially Uncontrolled Nonlinear Systems](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.08327)
*Ram Padmanabhan,Melkior Ornik*

Main category: math.OC

TL;DR: 提出一种在控制权限部分失效情况下，驱动受限非线性系统在有限时间内到达目标状态的技术，基于线性无漂移近似和时域分割方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 解决非线性系统在控制权限部分失效（部分输入不可控）情况下，仍需要在有限时间内精确到达目标状态的控制问题。这在飞行器、机器人等安全关键系统中尤为重要。

Method: 1. 在初始状态构建简单的线性无漂移近似系统；2. 将有限时间区间分割为逐渐减小的子区间；3. 在每个子区间基于近似动力学设计控制输入；4. 通过理论分析证明在时间区间长度受限条件下，这些输入能保证原非线性系统的有界误差。

Result: 证明了随着时间区间分割越来越细，即使存在不可控输入的影响，误差也能收敛到零。通过战斗机模型的仿真验证了所设计的控制输入序列能够在控制权限部分失效情况下实现目标状态。

Conclusion: 该方法为控制权限部分失效的受限非线性系统提供了有效的有限时间控制策略，通过线性近似和时域分割技术实现了误差收敛，并在实际系统中验证了有效性。

Abstract: This paper presents a technique to drive the state of a constrained nonlinear system to a specified target state in finite time, when the system suffers a partial loss in control authority. Our technique builds on a recent method to control constrained nonlinear systems by building a simple, linear driftless approximation at the initial state. We construct a partition of the finite time horizon into successively smaller intervals, and design controlled inputs based on the approximate dynamics in each partition. Under conditions that bound the length of the time horizon, we prove that these inputs result in bounded error from the target state in the original nonlinear system. As successive partitions of the time horizon become shorter, the error reduces to zero despite the effect of uncontrolled inputs. A simulation example on the model of a fighter jet demonstrates that the designed sequence of controlled inputs achieves the target state despite the system suffering a loss of control authority over one of its inputs.

</details>


### [67] [Data Poisoning Attacks Can Systematically Destabilize Data-Driven Control Synthesis](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.08392)
*Vijayanand Digge,Martina Vanelli,Ahmad W. Al-Dabbagh,Julien M. Hendrickx,Gianluca Bianchin*

Main category: math.OC

TL;DR: 论文揭示了数据驱动控制中的安全漏洞：攻击者可通过系统性的数据投毒，在无需知晓系统模型或控制器设计方法的情况下，使任何线性状态反馈控制器失稳物理系统。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 数据驱动控制通过直接利用数据合成控制器，绕过了显式模型辨识，但这种对数据的依赖引入了新的、尚未充分探索的安全漏洞。论文旨在揭示攻击者如何利用数据投毒破坏控制系统稳定性。

Method: 开发了一种递归数据投毒机制，通过生成伪造的状态轨迹，诱导系统动态特性的精确几何偏移。攻击者无需知晓系统模型或控制器合成过程，仅需在数据层面操作。

Result: 攻击者能够系统性地毒化用于控制合成的数据，导致任何由规划器合成的线性状态反馈控制器使物理系统失稳。数值模拟在无噪声和有噪声数据情况下均验证了这一发现。

Conclusion: 数据驱动控制流程可能被仅作用于数据层面的、模型无关的攻击确定性地破坏稳定性。这揭示了数据驱动控制范式中的根本性安全脆弱性，需要开发相应的防御机制。

Abstract: Data-driven control has emerged as a powerful paradigm for synthesizing controllers directly from data, bypassing explicit model identification. However, this reliance on data introduces new and largely unexplored vulnerabilities. In this paper, we show that an attacker can systematically poison the data used for control synthesis, causing any linear state-feedback controller synthesized by the planner to destabilize the physical system. Concerningly, we show that the attacker can achieve this objective without knowledge of the system model or the controller synthesis procedure. To this end, we develop a recursive data-poisoning mechanism that generates falsified state trajectories, inducing a precise geometric shift in the apparent system dynamics. More broadly, our results establish that data-driven control pipelines can be deterministically destabilized by model-agnostic attacks operating solely at the data level. Numerical simulations corroborate these findings for both noise-free and noisy data.

</details>


### [68] [Collective deterrence as a classification problem: Voting rules, deterrence credibility, and escalation risk](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.08482)
*Torgeir Aambø*

Main category: math.OC

TL;DR: 研究威慑联盟的机构设计选择，通过信号模型分析可信威慑与升级风险之间的权衡，构建二元分类问题评估不同制度设计在多种环境下的表现。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 集体拥有威慑技术的威慑联盟需要机构设计来决定何时对攻击或事件进行报复。这种制度设计的选择引入了可信威慑与升级风险之间的权衡，需要系统分析。

Method: 使用简单的信号模型研究这一权衡，构建相关的二元分类问题，通过经验ROC曲线分析各种选择函数和概率分布在四成员小联盟中的表现。

Result: 计算并研究了四成员联盟中各种选择函数和报复与误报概率分布的经验ROC曲线统计特性，识别出在不同环境下表现良好的制度设计。

Conclusion: 威慑联盟的制度设计需要在可信威慑与升级风险之间进行权衡，通过信号模型和分类问题分析可以识别出在不同环境下表现最优的制度设计方案。

Abstract: Deterrence coalitions that collectively own their deterrence technology, need an institutional design to decide when to retaliate against an attack or incident. This choice of institutional design, formalized through a social choice function, introduces a tradeoff between credible deterrence and escalation risk. We study this tradeoff via a simple signalling model, and use it to construct an associated binary classification problem to determine institutional designs that perform well in a variety of environments. For a small coalition of four members, we compute and study the statistics of the empirical ROC curves associated to a variety of choice functions and probability distributions for retaliation and false positives.

</details>


### [69] [Density-Driven Optimal Control: Convergence Guarantees for Stochastic LTI Multi-Agent Systems](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.08495)
*Kooktae Lee*

Main category: math.OC

TL;DR: 提出随机密度驱动最优控制(D²OC)方法，解决多智能体系统的去中心化非均匀区域覆盖问题，通过最小化Wasserstein距离作为运行成本，确保时间平均经验分布收敛到非参数目标密度。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有基于密度的方法通常依赖计算量大的欧拉PDE求解器或启发式规划，难以在具有高空间优先级和资源约束的任务中实现高效覆盖。需要一种能够桥接个体智能体动力学与集体分布匹配的严格拉格朗日框架。

Method: 提出随机密度驱动最优控制(D²OC)，这是一个严格的拉格朗日框架，将问题表述为类似随机MPC的优化问题，以Wasserstein距离作为运行成本进行最小化。该方法在随机LTI动力学下，确保时间平均经验分布收敛到非参数目标密度，并通过可达性分析建立形式化收敛保证。

Result: 数值结果表明，随机D²OC实现了鲁棒的去中心化覆盖，在最优性和一致性方面优于先前的启发式方法。关键贡献是通过可达性分析建立了形式化收敛保证，即使在存在过程和测量噪声的情况下也能提供有界的跟踪误差。

Conclusion: 随机D²OC为多智能体系统的去中心化非均匀区域覆盖问题提供了一个严格的理论框架，通过桥接个体动力学与集体分布匹配，实现了在噪声环境下的鲁棒覆盖性能，优于现有启发式方法。

Abstract: This paper addresses the decentralized non-uniform area coverage problem for multi-agent systems, a critical task in missions with high spatial priority and resource constraints. While existing density-based methods often rely on computationally heavy Eulerian PDE solvers or heuristic planning, we propose Stochastic Density-Driven Optimal Control (D$^2$OC). This is a rigorous Lagrangian framework that bridges the gap between individual agent dynamics and collective distribution matching. By formulating a stochastic MPC-like problem that minimizes the Wasserstein distance as a running cost, our approach ensures that the time-averaged empirical distribution converges to a non-parametric target density under stochastic LTI dynamics. A key contribution is the formal convergence guarantee established via reachability analysis, providing a bounded tracking error even in the presence of process and measurement noise. Numerical results verify that Stochastic D$^2$OC achieves robust, decentralized coverage while outperforming previous heuristic methods in optimality and consistency.

</details>

### [70] [An Analysis of Artificial Intelligence Adoption in NIH-Funded Research](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.07424)
*Navapat Nananukul,Mayank Kejriwal*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 利用大语言模型分析NIH资助的58,746个生物医学研究项目，发现AI项目占15.9%且获得13.4%的资金溢价，但存在研究到部署的严重差距，且健康差异研究代表性不足。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 了解NIH投资组合中人工智能和机器学习采用情况对于研究资助策略、机构规划和健康政策至关重要。大语言模型的出现从根本上改变了研究景观分析，使研究人员能够从数千个非结构化研究文档中进行大规模语义提取。

Method: 提出了一种人机协同研究方法，利用大语言模型自动分类和总结大规模研究描述。该方法应用于分析2025年NIH资助的58,746个生物医学研究项目。

Result: 1) AI项目占NIH投资组合的15.9%，获得13.4%的资金溢价，主要集中在发现、预测和数据整合领域；2) 存在严重的研究到部署差距，79%的AI项目停留在研究/开发阶段，仅14.7%涉及临床部署或实施；3) 健康差异研究严重不足，仅占AI资助工作的5.7%，与NIH的公平使命不符。

Conclusion: 这些发现为基于证据的政策干预建立了框架，旨在使NIH的AI投资组合与健康公平目标和战略研究重点保持一致。

Abstract: Understanding the landscape of artificial intelligence (AI) and machine learning (ML) adoption across the National Institutes of Health (NIH) portfolio is critical for research funding strategy, institutional planning, and health policy. The advent of large language models (LLMs) has fundamentally transformed research landscape analysis, enabling researchers to perform large-scale semantic extraction from thousands of unstructured research documents. In this paper, we illustrate a human-in-the-loop research methodology for LLMs to automatically classify and summarize research descriptions at scale. Using our methodology, we present a comprehensive analysis of 58,746 NIH-funded biomedical research projects from 2025. We show that: (1) AI constitutes 15.9% of the NIH portfolio with a 13.4% funding premium, concentrated in discovery, prediction, and data integration across disease domains; (2) a critical research-to-deployment gap exists, with 79% of AI projects remaining in research/development stages while only 14.7% engage in clinical deployment or implementation; and (3) health disparities research is severely underrepresented at just 5.7% of AI-funded work despite its importance to NIH's equity mission. These findings establish a framework for evidence-based policy interventions to align the NIH AI portfolio with health equity goals and strategic research priorities.

</details>


### [71] [ConsistRM: Improving Generative Reward Models via Consistency-Aware Self-Training](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.07484)
*Yu Liang,Liangxin Liu,Longzheng Wang,Yan Wang,Yueyang Zhang,Long Xia,Zhiyuan Sun,Daiting Shi*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: ConsistRM：一种无需人工标注的自训练框架，通过一致性感知奖励解决生成式奖励模型的可扩展性和稳定性问题


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 生成式奖励模型（GRMs）在表示能力和灵活性上优于传统标量奖励模型，但面临两大挑战：1）依赖昂贵的人工标注数据限制了可扩展性；2）自训练方法存在不稳定性和奖励攻击脆弱性。需要开发无需人工标注且稳定的GRM训练方法。

Method: 提出ConsistRM自训练框架，包含两个核心组件：1）一致性感知答案奖励：通过时间一致性生成可靠的伪标签，提供稳定的模型优化；2）一致性感知批评奖励：评估多个批评之间的语义一致性，分配细粒度和差异化的奖励。

Result: 在五个基准数据集和四个基础模型上的实验表明，ConsistRM平均优于传统强化微调（RFT）1.5%。进一步分析显示，ConsistRM增强了输出一致性，缓解了由输入顺序引起的位置偏差，证明了一致性感知奖励在改进GRMs方面的有效性。

Conclusion: ConsistRM通过一致性感知奖励机制，成功解决了GRMs训练中的可扩展性和稳定性问题，为无需人工标注的LLM对齐提供了有效解决方案，显著提升了模型性能并增强了输出一致性。

Abstract: Generative reward models (GRMs) have emerged as a promising approach for aligning Large Language Models (LLMs) with human preferences by offering greater representational capacity and flexibility than traditional scalar reward models. However, GRMs face two major challenges: reliance on costly human-annotated data restricts scalability, and self-training approaches often suffer from instability and vulnerability to reward hacking. To address these issues, we propose ConsistRM, a self-training framework that enables effective and stable GRM training without human annotations. ConsistRM incorporates the Consistency-Aware Answer Reward, which produces reliable pseudo-labels with temporal consistency, thereby providing more stable model optimization. Moreover, the Consistency-Aware Critique Reward is introduced to assess semantic consistency across multiple critiques and allocates fine-grained and differentiated rewards. Experiments on five benchmark datasets across four base models demonstrate that ConsistRM outperforms vanilla Reinforcement Fine-Tuning (RFT) by an average of 1.5%. Further analysis shows that ConsistRM enhances output consistency and mitigates position bias caused by input order, highlighting the effectiveness of consistency-aware rewards in improving GRMs.

</details>


### [72] [ReflectRM: Boosting Generative Reward Models via Self-Reflection within a Unified Judgment Framework](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.07506)
*Kai Qin,Liangxin Liu,Yu Liang,Longzheng Wang,Yan Wang,Yueyang Zhang,Long Xia,Zhiyuan Sun,Houde Liu,Daiting Shi*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: ReflectRM是一种新型生成式奖励模型，通过自我反思机制评估分析过程质量，在统一生成框架下联合建模响应偏好和分析偏好，显著提升对齐性能并减少位置偏差。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有生成式奖励模型主要关注结果层面的监督，忽视了分析过程质量，这限制了其潜力。需要一种能够评估分析质量并增强偏好建模的方法。

Method: 提出ReflectRM，利用自我反思评估分析质量，在统一生成框架下联合建模响应偏好和分析偏好。推理时通过自我反思识别最可靠的分析，从中得出最终偏好预测。

Result: 在四个基准测试中，ReflectRM持续提升性能，在Qwen3-4B上平均准确率增益+3.7。响应偏好和分析偏好相互增强，显著缓解位置偏差，相比领先GRMs提升+10.2，成为更稳定的评估器。

Conclusion: ReflectRM通过自我反思机制有效提升生成式奖励模型的性能，联合建模响应和分析偏好，显著减少位置偏差，为LLM对齐提供了更可靠的评估框架。

Abstract: Reward Models (RMs) are critical components in the Reinforcement Learning from Human Feedback (RLHF) pipeline, directly determining the alignment quality of Large Language Models (LLMs). Recently, Generative Reward Models (GRMs) have emerged as a superior paradigm, offering higher interpretability and stronger generalization than traditional scalar RMs. However, existing methods for GRMs focus primarily on outcome-level supervision, neglecting analytical process quality, which constrains their potential. To address this, we propose ReflectRM, a novel GRM that leverages self-reflection to assess analytical quality and enhance preference modeling. ReflectRM is trained under a unified generative framework for joint modeling of response preference and analysis preference. During inference, we use its self-reflection capability to identify the most reliable analysis, from which the final preference prediction is derived. Experiments across four benchmarks show that ReflectRM consistently improves performance, achieving an average accuracy gain of +3.7 on Qwen3-4B. Further experiments confirm that response preference and analysis preference are mutually reinforcing. Notably, ReflectRM substantially mitigates positional bias, yielding +10.2 improvement compared with leading GRMs and establishing itself as a more stable evaluator.

</details>


### [73] [Trust the AI, Doubt Yourself: The Effect of Urgency on Self-Confidence in Human-AI Interaction](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.07535)
*Baran Shajari,Xiaoran Liu,Kyanna Dagenais,Istvan David*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 研究发现AI交互中的紧迫感虽不影响对AI的信任，但会损害用户自信和自我效能感，可能导致长期性能下降和决策失误


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 研究动机是探讨人机交互中常被忽视的紧迫感因素，特别是当AI系统向用户寻求建议或指导时产生的紧迫感对用户心理和行为的影响

Method: 通过实验方法，招募30名人类参与者，研究在不同紧迫感条件下的人机交互场景，分析紧迫感对用户信任、自信和自我效能感的影响

Result: 实验结果显示：1）人机交互中的紧迫感不影响用户对AI的信任；2）紧迫感会损害用户的自我信心和自我效能感；3）用户在有准备的情况下接触人机系统时比无准备时更有信心

Conclusion: 紧迫感在人机交互中具有重要影响，虽然不影响对AI的信任，但会削弱用户自信，可能导致长期性能损失、次优决策和人为错误，最终影响AI系统的可持续性。建议软件工程师和决策者应让用户逐步适应人机系统而非突然暴露

Abstract: Studies show that interactions with an AI system fosters trust in human users towards AI. An often overlooked element of such interaction dynamics is the (sense of) urgency when the human user is prompted by an AI agent, e.g., for advice or guidance. In this paper, we show that although the presence of urgency in human-AI interactions does not affect the trust in AI, it may be detrimental to the human user's self-confidence and self-efficacy. In the long run, the loss of confidence may lead to performance loss, suboptimal decisions, human errors, and ultimately, unsustainable AI systems. Our evidence comes from an experiment with 30 human participants. Our results indicate that users may feel more confident in their work when they are eased into the human-AI setup rather than exposed to it without preparation. We elaborate on the implications of this finding for software engineers and decision-makers.

</details>


### [74] [Dual-Loop Control in DCVerse: Advancing Reliable Deployment of AI in Data Centers via Digital Twins](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.07559)
*Qingang Zhang,Yuejun Yan,Guangyu Wu,Siew-Chien Wong,Jimin Jia,Zhaoyang Wang,Yonggang Wen*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 论文提出基于数字孪生的双环控制框架DLCF，用于解决数据中心能效与故障风险平衡问题，通过数字孪生、策略库和双环机制实现安全可靠的DRL部署，在实际数据中心冷却系统中验证了4.09%的节能效果。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现代数据中心规模日益扩大和复杂化，在平衡能源效率与故障风险方面面临重大挑战。深度强化学习虽在智能控制方面潜力巨大，但在任务关键系统中的部署受到数据稀缺和缺乏实时预评估机制的限制。

Method: 提出双环控制框架DLCF，包含物理系统、数字孪生和多样化DRL策略库三个核心实体。通过包含实时数据采集、数据同化、DRL策略训练、预评估和专家验证的双环机制实现交互。基于该框架开发了DCVerse平台。

Result: 在实际数据中心冷却系统的案例研究中验证，相比传统控制策略实现了高达4.09%的节能效果，且未违反SLA要求。框架还提高了策略可解释性，支持更可信的DRL部署。

Conclusion: DLCF为数据中心可靠的基于AI的控制提供了基础，理论分析表明其能提升样本效率、泛化能力、安全性和最优性。未来可扩展至系统级的整体优化。

Abstract: The growing scale and complexity of modern data centers present major challenges in balancing energy efficiency with outage risk. Although Deep Reinforcement Learning (DRL) shows strong potential for intelligent control, its deployment in mission-critical systems is limited by data scarcity and the lack of real-time pre-evaluation mechanisms. This paper introduces the Dual-Loop Control Framework (DLCF), a digital twin-based architecture designed to overcome these challenges. The framework comprises three core entities: the physical system, a digital twin, and a policy reservoir of diverse DRL agents. These components interact through a dual-loop mechanism involving real-time data acquisition, data assimilation, DRL policy training, pre-evaluation, and expert verification. Theoretical analysis shows how DLCF can improve sample efficiency, generalization, safety, and optimality. Leveraging DLCF, we implemented the DCVerse platform and validated it through case studies on a real-world data center cooling system. The evaluation shows that our approach achieves up to 4.09% energy savings over conventional control strategies without violating SLA requirements. Additionally, the framework improves policy interpretability and supports more trustworthy DRL deployment. This work provides a foundation for reliable AI-based control in data centers and points toward future extensions for holistic, system-wide optimization.

</details>


### [75] [From Papers to Property Tables: A Priority-Based LLM Workflow for Materials Data Extraction](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.07584)
*Koushik Rameshbabu,Jing Luo,Ali Shargh,Khalid A. El-Awady,Jaafar A. El-Awady*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 提出基于大语言模型的层次化工作流，自动从科研论文中提取和重构结构化实验数据，以合金层裂强度为案例，实现高精度数据提取。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 科学数据分散在科研论文的文本、表格和图表中，报告方式不一致，导致手动提取和聚合数据缓慢且易出错，需要自动化解决方案。

Method: 采用提示驱动的层次化工作流，使用大语言模型从全文论文中提取数据，应用三级优先级策略：T1从文本/表格直接提取，T2基于物理公式推导，T3从图表数字化提取，并进行单位标准化和物理一致性验证。

Result: 在30篇论文的11,967个数据点上评估，整体加权准确率达94.69%，优先级准确率分别为：T1 94.93%、T2 92.04%、T3 83.49%。API接口实现显示方法具有可扩展性。

Conclusion: 该工作流展示了无需任务特定微调即可将非结构化技术文献转换为可追溯、分析就绪数据集的实用方法，为材料科学领域实现可扩展数据库构建提供了可行方案。

Abstract: Scientific data are widely dispersed across research articles and are often reported inconsistently across text, tables, and figures, making manual data extraction and aggregation slow and error-prone. We present a prompt-driven, hierarchical workflow that uses a large language model (LLM) to automatically extract and reconstruct structured, shot-level shock-physics experimental records by integrating information distributed across text, tables, figures, and physics-based derivations from full-text published research articles, using alloy spall strength as a representative case study. The pipeline targeted 37 experimentally relevant fields per shot and applied a three-level priority strategy: (T1) direct extraction from text/tables, (T2) physics-based derivation using verified governing relations, and (T3) digitization from figures when necessary. Extracted values were normalized to canonical units, tagged by priority for traceability, and validated with physics-based consistency and plausibility checks. Evaluated on a benchmark of 30 published research articles comprising 11,967 evaluated data points, the workflow achieved high overall accuracy, with priority-wise accuracies of 94.93% (T1), 92.04% (T2), and 83.49% (T3), and an overall weighted accuracy of 94.69%. Cross-model testing further indicated strong agreement for text/table and equation-derived fields, with lower agreement for figure-based extraction. Implementation through an API interface demonstrated the scalability of the approach, achieving consistent extraction performance and, in a subset of test cases, matching or exceeding chat-based accuracy. This workflow demonstrates a practical approach for converting unstructured technical literature into traceable, analysis-ready datasets without task-specific fine-tuning, enabling scalable database construction in materials science.

</details>


### [76] [Too long; didn't solve](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.07593)
*Lucía M. Cabrera,Isaac Saxton-Knight*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 研究发现数学问题提示长度和解答长度与大型语言模型失败率呈正相关，结构长度与经验难度相关


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 数学基准测试广泛用于评估大型语言模型的推理能力，但对其结构特性如何影响模型行为知之甚少。本研究旨在探究提示长度和解答长度这两个结构变量如何影响模型在专家编写的对抗性数学问题数据集上的表现。

Method: 构建了一个专家编写的对抗性数学问题数据集，分析两个结构长度变量（提示长度和解答长度）与模型性能的关系。进行了主要分析（模型失败率与长度的相关性）和次要探索性分析（跨模型分歧）。在难度调整的标准化分析中检验了这些变量与模型分离度的关系。

Result: 发现提示长度和解答长度都与模型失败率呈正相关。在难度调整的标准化分析中，两个变量与实现的模型分离度保持弱负相关，其中提示长度的相关性稍强。主要稳健发现是结构长度与该数据集中的经验难度相关。

Conclusion: 数学问题的结构长度（特别是提示长度和解答长度）是影响大型语言模型性能的重要因素，与模型失败率正相关，表明结构复杂度增加了模型的推理难度。

Abstract: Mathematical benchmarks consisting of a range of mathematics problems are widely used to evaluate the reasoning abilities of large language models, yet little is known about how their structural properties influence model behaviour. In this work, we investigate two structural length variables, prompt length and solution length, and analyse how they relate to model performance on a newly constructed adversarial dataset of expert-authored mathematics problems. We find that both prompt and solution lengths correlate positively with increased model failure across models. We also include a secondary, exploratory analysis of cross-model disagreement. Under a difficulty-adjusted normalised analysis, both variables retain weak negative associations with realised model separation, slightly stronger for prompt length. Overall, our main robust finding is that structural length is linked to empirical difficulty in this dataset.

</details>


### [77] [Reasoning Graphs: Deterministic Agent Accuracy through Evidence-Centric Chain-of-Thought Feedback](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.07595)
*Matthew Penaroza*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 该论文提出推理图和检索图，通过图结构持久化智能体的推理链，实现证据中心的反馈机制，无需重新训练即可提升语言模型智能体的准确率并降低方差。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当前语言模型智能体对每个查询都从头开始推理，每次检索证据和思考后，思维链被丢弃，导致相似查询无法利用先前洞察，造成准确率低、方差高的问题。

Method: 引入推理图（将智能体对每个证据的思维链持久化为结构化边）和检索图（为管道规划器提供输入以收紧候选集），通过图遍历算法实现证据中心的反馈机制，形成自我改进循环。

Result: 系统在连续运行中准确率提升且方差崩溃，所有决策可通过图完全追溯，改进无需重新训练，所有增益来自通过图遍历的上下文工程。

Conclusion: 推理图和检索图通过结构化记忆机制为语言模型智能体提供证据中心的反馈，显著改善多跳问答任务中的准确率收敛和方差控制，实现可追溯的自我改进。

Abstract: Language model agents reason from scratch on every query: each time an agent retrieves evidence and deliberates, the chain of thought is discarded and the next similar query starts with no prior insight. This produces lower accuracy and high variance, as the same type of query can succeed or fail unpredictably. We introduce reasoning graphs, a graph structure that persists an agent's per-evidence chain of thought as structured edges connected to the evidence items they evaluate. Unlike prior memory mechanisms that store distilled strategies as flat records indexed by query similarity or appended by recency, reasoning graphs enable evidence-centric feedback: given a new candidate set, the system traverses all incoming evaluation edges for each evidence item across all prior runs, surfacing how that specific item has been judged before. This backward traversal from evidence inward is a structurally different capability from query-similarity retrieval, because the feedback is tied to the specific evidence the agent is currently examining, not to the query. We further introduce retrieval graphs, a complementary structure that feeds a pipeline planner to tighten the candidate funnel over successive runs. Together, both graphs form a self-improving feedback loop: accuracy rises and variance collapses over successive runs, with every decision fully traceable through the graph. This improvement requires no retraining; the base model remains frozen and all gains come from context engineering via graph traversal. We formalize the graph structure, traversal algorithms, and feedback mechanisms, and describe a sequential cluster evaluation protocol for measuring accuracy convergence and variance collapse on multi-hop question answering benchmarks.

</details>


### [78] [How Independent are Large Language Models? A Statistical Framework for Auditing Behavioral Entanglement and Reweighting Verifier Ensembles](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.07650)
*Chenchen Kuai,Jiwan Jiang,Zihao Zhu,Hao Wang,Keshu Wu,Zihao Li,Yunlong Zhang,Chenxi Liu,Zhengzhong Tu,Zhiwen Fan,Yang Zhou*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 该研究开发了一个统计框架来审计黑盒大语言模型之间的行为纠缠，揭示了看似独立的模型实际上存在隐藏的依赖关系，这会影响多模型系统的可靠性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 大语言模型生态系统的快速增长引发了一个关键问题：看似多样化的模型是否真正独立？共享的预训练数据、知识蒸馏和对齐流程可能导致隐藏的行为依赖关系（潜在纠缠），这会破坏多模型系统（如LLM-as-a-judge流水线和集成验证）的可靠性，因为这些系统隐含地假设了独立信号。

Method: 开发了一个统计框架来审计黑盒LLM之间的行为纠缠。该方法引入了一个多分辨率层次结构，通过两个信息论指标来表征联合失败流形：（1）难度加权行为纠缠指数，放大简单任务上的同步失败；（2）累积信息增益指标，捕捉错误响应的方向性对齐。在来自六个模型家族的18个LLM上进行了广泛实验。

Result: 发现了广泛的行为纠缠现象，并分析了其对LLM-as-a-judge评估的影响。CIG指标与评判精度下降存在统计显著关联：GPT-4o-mini的Spearman系数为0.64（p<0.001），Llama3-based评判者的系数为0.71（p<0.01），表明更强的依赖对应着增加的过度认可偏差。通过去纠缠验证器集成重新加权，实现了比多数投票高达4.5%的准确率提升。

Conclusion: 大语言模型之间存在显著的行为纠缠，这会影响多模型系统的可靠性。提出的统计框架能够有效检测这种纠缠，并且通过去纠缠集成重新加权可以减轻相关偏差，提高验证性能。这对依赖多个LLM的评估和验证系统具有重要意义。

Abstract: The rapid growth of the large language model (LLM) ecosystem raises a critical question: are seemingly diverse models truly independent? Shared pretraining data, distillation, and alignment pipelines can induce hidden behavioral dependencies, latent entanglement, that undermine multi-model systems such as LLM-as-a-judge pipelines and ensemble verification, which implicitly assume independent signals. In practice, this manifests as correlated reasoning patterns and synchronized failures, where apparent agreement reflects shared error modes rather than independent validation. To address this, we develop a statistical framework for auditing behavioral entanglement among black-box LLMs. Our approach introduces a multi-resolution hierarchy that characterizes the joint failure manifold through two information-theoretic metrics: (i) a Difficulty-Weighted Behavioral Entanglement Index, which amplifies synchronized failures on easy tasks, and (ii) a Cumulative Information Gain (CIG) metric, which captures directional alignment in erroneous responses. Through extensive experiments on 18 LLMs from six model families, we identify widespread behavioral entanglement and analyze its impact on LLM-as-a-judge evaluation. We find that CIG exhibits a statistically significant association with degradation in judge precision, with Spearman coefficient of 0.64 (p < 0.001) for GPT-4o-mini and 0.71 (p < 0.01) for Llama3-based judges, indicating that stronger dependency corresponds to increased over-endorsement bias. Finally, we demonstrate a practical use case of entanglement through de-entangled verifier ensemble reweighting. By adjusting model contributions based on inferred independence, the proposed method mitigates correlated bias and improves verification performance, achieving up to a 4.5% accuracy gain over majority voting.

</details>


### [79] [Bridging Natural Language and Interactive What-If Interfaces via LLM-Generated Declarative Specification](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.07652)
